you'll freeze!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be affected by extreme cold."],"tags":["informal","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Since the last update, the program freezes after a few minutes of use.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of a machine or system, to come to a sudden halt, to stop working (functioning)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"freeze up"},{"word":"grind to a halt"},{"word":"hang"},{"word":"lock up"},{"word":"seize"},{"word":"seize up"}],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences","software"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Despite all of the rehearsals, I froze as soon as I got on stage.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of a person or other animal, to stop (become motionless) or be stopped due to attentiveness, fear, surprise, etc."],"synonyms":[{"word":"freeze up"}],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To cause someone to become motionless."],"synonyms":[{"word":"halt"},{"word":"immobilize"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Over time, he froze towards her, and ceased to react to her friendly advances.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To lose or cause to lose warmth of feeling;
